# Locations described without a Map action

Audit date: 2026-10-29.

Scanned 15,243 entries. Fixed and verified 5103 map/achievement actions; 0 spatial descriptions remain without an action.

Verified 47,989 character contexts through both Collections and Favorites. Both factions and distinct source/quest class and race restrictions are exercised, including ineligible characters viewing another faction or class's collectible.

The initial audit omitted character identity and missed locations hidden by eligibility filtering. The compiled fallback now preserves those items' recorded destinations for inspection; acquiring the item and choosing a live quest destination still respect the character's restrictions.

Stored guide coordinates and pinned source records are compiled offline into a shared lookup table. Existing authored routes and character quest progression take priority. Named zones without coordinates open the source area and receive no invented center pin. Instance-only coordinates identify the entrance. The Map button continues to open a rewarding achievement inside the addon for achievement mounts. Patch headings and achievement names alone are not travel instructions.

| Type | Fixed and verified actions |
| --- | ---: |
| mounts | 107 |
| cosmetics | 509 |
| achievements | 4358 |
| toys | 69 |
| pets | 60 |

## Character restrictions missed by the original audit

These entries already had destinations without a character identity, but lost them in at least one faction, class or race context. They now retain inspection destinations or the rewarding achievement action.

| Type | Additional entries fixed |
| --- | ---: |
| cosmetics | 478 |
| toys | 41 |
| mounts | 90 |
| pets | 19 |

## Explicit exclusions

- Achievement: Cataclysm factions and reputation totals (5374): Shared reputation tips mention Tol Barad; the total-reputation milestone has no single required destination.
- Achievement: Cataclysm factions and reputation totals (5723): Shared reputation tips mention Tol Barad; the total-reputation milestone has no single required destination.
- Ravenous Shalewing (3543): No implemented or verified acquisition source; the old journal's place label does not establish a collectible spawn.
- Shalewing Devourer (3544): No implemented or verified acquisition source; the old journal's place label does not establish a collectible spawn.
- Titan Orb (4725): Unimplemented Titan Orb has no obtainable source; its description distinguishes it from a different pet.
